It can replace traditional user management by relying on an IdP for authenticating users and ensures secure session handling through cookie sharing between the Vouch Proxy server and the applications it protects.

 - Vouch Proxy protects multiple websites using OAuth and OIDC for authentication.
 - It allows users to log in once and access various applications for a set period.
 - Vouch Proxy can replace the need for separate user management in web applications.

Source: https://kitploit.com/en/tools/github/vouch/vouch-proxy

The data reflects a successful shift towards natural habitat restoration after years of intensive farming.

 - Butterfly and bumblebee populations have shown remarkable increases of 117% and 64%, respectively.

Category: Business
Importance: 6
Retailers employ price-matching guarantees to appear consumer-friendly while potentially keeping prices artificially high. These guarantees are often suspended during major sales events like Black Friday, when significant discounts are typically offered. The economic theory suggests that such practices can deter price competition among rival stores, leading to overall higher prices for consumers.

 - Price-matching guarantees can discourage competitive pricing among retailers.
 - Retailers tend to suspend price matching during major sales events, indicating a strategic choice.

Source: https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/the-hidden-math-behind-this-paradoxical-black-friday-sale-strategy/

---


Category: Business
Importance: 6
Retailers often utilize price-matching guarantees to maintain higher price levels during competitive shopping events like Black Friday, which can paradoxically lead to elevated prices for consumers. These guarantees can prevent price cuts among competitors, ensuring that both stores maintain similar pricing and profitability instead of engaging in a downward price competition.

 - Price-matching guarantees can keep retail prices high instead of lowering them as intended.
 - Retailers often suspend these guarantees during significant sales events, indicating their true purpose is to prevent price competition.

Source: https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/the-hidden-math-behind-this-paradoxical-black-friday-sale-strategy/
